#include "main.h"
#include "tx.h"
#include "fw.h"
#include "ps.h"
#include "debug.h"

static
void rtw_tx_stats(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, struct ieee80211_vif *vif,
		  struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct ieee80211_hdr *hdr;
	struct rtw_vif *rtwvif;

	hdr = (struct ieee80211_hdr *)skb->data;

	if (!ieee80211_is_data(hdr->frame_control))
		return;

	if (!is_broadcast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1) &&
	    !is_multicast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1)) {
		rtwdev->stats.tx_unicast += skb->len;
		rtwdev->stats.tx_cnt++;
		if (vif) {
			rtwvif = (struct rtw_vif *)vif->drv_priv;
			rtwvif->stats.tx_unicast += skb->len;
			rtwvif->stats.tx_cnt++;
		}
	}
}

void rtw_tx_fill_tx_desc(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
			 struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info, struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct rtw_tx_desc *tx_desc = (struct rtw_tx_desc *)skb->data;
	bool more_data = false;

	if (pkt_info->qsel == TX_DESC_QSEL_HIGH)
		more_data = true;

	tx_desc->w0 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->tx_pkt_size, RTW_TX_DESC_W0_TXPKTSIZE) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->offset, RTW_TX_DESC_W0_OFFSET) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->bmc, RTW_TX_DESC_W0_BMC) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->ls, RTW_TX_DESC_W0_LS) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->dis_qselseq, RTW_TX_DESC_W0_DISQSELSEQ);

	tx_desc->w1 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->mac_id, RTW_TX_DESC_W1_MACID) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->qsel, RTW_TX_DESC_W1_QSEL) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->rate_id, RTW_TX_DESC_W1_RATE_ID) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->sec_type, RTW_TX_DESC_W1_SEC_TYPE) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->pkt_offset, RTW_TX_DESC_W1_PKT_OFFSET) |
		      le32_encode_bits(more_data, RTW_TX_DESC_W1_MORE_DATA);

	tx_desc->w2 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->ampdu_en, RTW_TX_DESC_W2_AGG_EN) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->report, RTW_TX_DESC_W2_SPE_RPT) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->ampdu_density, RTW_TX_DESC_W2_AMPDU_DEN) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->bt_null, RTW_TX_DESC_W2_BT_NULL);

	tx_desc->w3 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->hw_ssn_sel, RTW_TX_DESC_W3_HW_SSN_SEL) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->use_rate, RTW_TX_DESC_W3_USE_RATE) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->dis_rate_fallback, RTW_TX_DESC_W3_DISDATAFB) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->rts, RTW_TX_DESC_W3_USE_RTS) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->nav_use_hdr, RTW_TX_DESC_W3_NAVUSEHDR) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->ampdu_factor, RTW_TX_DESC_W3_MAX_AGG_NUM);

	tx_desc->w4 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->rate, RTW_TX_DESC_W4_DATARATE);

	if (rtwdev->chip->old_datarate_fb_limit)
		tx_desc->w4 |= le32_encode_bits(0x1f, RTW_TX_DESC_W4_DATARATE_FB_LIMIT);

	tx_desc->w5 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->short_gi, RTW_TX_DESC_W5_DATA_SHORT) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->bw, RTW_TX_DESC_W5_DATA_BW) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->ldpc, RTW_TX_DESC_W5_DATA_LDPC) |
		      le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->stbc, RTW_TX_DESC_W5_DATA_STBC);

	tx_desc->w6 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->sn, RTW_TX_DESC_W6_SW_DEFINE);

	tx_desc->w8 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->en_hwseq, RTW_TX_DESC_W8_EN_HWSEQ);

	tx_desc->w9 = le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->seq, RTW_TX_DESC_W9_SW_SEQ);

	if (pkt_info->rts) {
		tx_desc->w4 |= le32_encode_bits(DESC_RATE24M, RTW_TX_DESC_W4_RTSRATE);
		tx_desc->w5 |= le32_encode_bits(1, RTW_TX_DESC_W5_DATA_RTS_SHORT);
	}

	if (pkt_info->tim_offset)
		tx_desc->w9 |= le32_encode_bits(1, RTW_TX_DESC_W9_TIM_EN) |
			       le32_encode_bits(pkt_info->tim_offset, RTW_TX_DESC_W9_TIM_OFFSET);
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(rtw_tx_fill_tx_desc);

static u8 get_tx_ampdu_factor(struct ieee80211_sta *sta)
{
	u8 exp = sta->deflink.ht_cap.ampdu_factor;

	/* the least ampdu factor is 8K, and the value in the tx desc is the
	 * max aggregation num, which represents val * 2 packets can be
	 * aggregated in an AMPDU, so here we should use 8/2=4 as the base
	 */
	return (BIT(2) << exp) - 1;
}

static u8 get_tx_ampdu_density(struct ieee80211_sta *sta)
{
	return sta->deflink.ht_cap.ampdu_density;
}

static u8 get_highest_ht_tx_rate(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				 struct ieee80211_sta *sta)
{
	u8 rate;

	if (rtwdev->hal.rf_type == RF_2T2R && sta->deflink.ht_cap.mcs.rx_mask[1] != 0)
		rate = DESC_RATEMCS15;
	else
		rate = DESC_RATEMCS7;

	return rate;
}

static u8 get_highest_vht_tx_rate(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				  struct ieee80211_sta *sta)
{
	struct rtw_efuse *efuse = &rtwdev->efuse;
	u8 rate;
	u16 tx_mcs_map;

	tx_mcs_map = le16_to_cpu(sta->deflink.vht_cap.vht_mcs.tx_mcs_map);
	if (efuse->hw_cap.nss == 1) {
		switch (tx_mcs_map & 0x3) {
		case IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_7:
			rate = DESC_RATEVHT1SS_MCS7;
			break;
		case IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_8:
			rate = DESC_RATEVHT1SS_MCS8;
			break;
		default:
		case IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9:
			rate = DESC_RATEVHT1SS_MCS9;
			break;
		}
	} else if (efuse->hw_cap.nss >= 2) {
		switch ((tx_mcs_map & 0xc) >> 2) {
		case IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_7:
			rate = DESC_RATEVHT2SS_MCS7;
			break;
		case IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_8:
			rate = DESC_RATEVHT2SS_MCS8;
			break;
		default:
		case IEEE80211_VHT_MCS_SUPPORT_0_9:
			rate = DESC_RATEVHT2SS_MCS9;
			break;
		}
	} else {
		rate = DESC_RATEVHT1SS_MCS9;
	}

	return rate;
}

static void rtw_tx_report_enable(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				 struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info)
{
	struct rtw_tx_report *tx_report = &rtwdev->tx_report;

	/* [11:8], reserved, fills with zero
	 * [7:2],  tx report sequence number
	 * [1:0],  firmware use, fills with zero
	 */
	pkt_info->sn = (atomic_inc_return(&tx_report->sn) << 2) & 0xfc;
	pkt_info->report = true;
}

void rtw_tx_report_purge_timer(struct timer_list *t)
{
	struct rtw_dev *rtwdev = timer_container_of(rtwdev, t,
						    tx_report.purge_timer);
	struct rtw_tx_report *tx_report = &rtwdev->tx_report;
	unsigned long flags;

	if (skb_queue_len(&tx_report->queue) == 0)
		return;

	rtw_warn(rtwdev, "failed to get tx report from firmware\n");

	spin_lock_irqsave(&tx_report->q_lock, flags);
	rtw88_candidate_log("rtw88: TX report expired pending=%u; ACK unknown\n",
                            skb_queue_len(&tx_report->queue));
	skb_queue_purge(&tx_report->queue);
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&tx_report->q_lock, flags);
}

void rtw_tx_report_enqueue(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, struct sk_buff *skb, u8 sn)
{
	struct rtw_tx_report *tx_report = &rtwdev->tx_report;
	unsigned long flags;
	u8 *drv_data;

	/* pass sn to tx report handler through driver data */
	drv_data = (u8 *)IEEE80211_SKB_CB(skb)->status.status_driver_data;
	*drv_data = sn;

	spin_lock_irqsave(&tx_report->q_lock, flags);
	__skb_queue_tail(&tx_report->queue, skb);
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&tx_report->q_lock, flags);

	mod_timer(&tx_report->purge_timer, jiffies + RTW_TX_PROBE_TIMEOUT);
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(rtw_tx_report_enqueue);

static void rtw_tx_report_tx_status(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				    struct sk_buff *skb, bool acked)
{
	struct ieee80211_tx_info *info;

	info = IEEE80211_SKB_CB(skb);
	ieee80211_tx_info_clear_status(info);
	if (acked)
		info->flags |= IEEE80211_TX_STAT_ACK;
	else
		info->flags &= ~IEEE80211_TX_STAT_ACK;

	ieee80211_tx_status_irqsafe(rtwdev->hw, skb);
}

void rtw_tx_report_handle(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, struct sk_buff *skb, int src)
{
	struct rtw_tx_report *tx_report = &rtwdev->tx_report;
	struct rtw_c2h_cmd *c2h;
	struct sk_buff *cur, *tmp;
	unsigned long flags;
	u8 sn, st;
	u8 *n;

	c2h = get_c2h_from_skb(skb);

	if (src == C2H_CCX_TX_RPT) {
		sn = GET_CCX_REPORT_SEQNUM_V0(c2h->payload);
		st = GET_CCX_REPORT_STATUS_V0(c2h->payload);
	} else {
		sn = GET_CCX_REPORT_SEQNUM_V1(c2h->payload);
		st = GET_CCX_REPORT_STATUS_V1(c2h->payload);
	}

	spin_lock_irqsave(&tx_report->q_lock, flags);
	skb_queue_walk_safe(&tx_report->queue, cur, tmp) {
		n = (u8 *)IEEE80211_SKB_CB(cur)->status.status_driver_data;
		if (*n == sn) {
			__skb_unlink(cur, &tx_report->queue);
			rtw_tx_report_tx_status(rtwdev, cur, st == 0);
			break;
		}
	}
	spin_unlock_irqrestore(&tx_report->q_lock, flags);
}

static u8 rtw_get_mgmt_rate(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, struct sk_buff *skb,
			    u8 lowest_rate, bool ignore_rate)
{
	struct ieee80211_tx_info *tx_info = IEEE80211_SKB_CB(skb);
	struct ieee80211_vif *vif = tx_info->control.vif;
	bool force_lowest = test_bit(RTW_FLAG_FORCE_LOWEST_RATE, rtwdev->flags);

	if (!vif || !vif->bss_conf.basic_rates || ignore_rate || force_lowest)
		return lowest_rate;

	return __ffs(vif->bss_conf.basic_rates) + lowest_rate;
}

static void rtw_tx_pkt_info_update_rate(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
					struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
					struct sk_buff *skb,
					bool ignore_rate)
{
	if (rtwdev->hal.current_band_type == RTW_BAND_2G) {
		pkt_info->rate_id = RTW_RATEID_B_20M;
		pkt_info->rate = rtw_get_mgmt_rate(rtwdev, skb, DESC_RATE1M,
						   ignore_rate);
	} else {
		pkt_info->rate_id = RTW_RATEID_G;
		pkt_info->rate = rtw_get_mgmt_rate(rtwdev, skb, DESC_RATE6M,
						   ignore_rate);
	}

	pkt_info->use_rate = true;
	pkt_info->dis_rate_fallback = true;
}

static void rtw_tx_pkt_info_update_sec(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				       struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
				       struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct ieee80211_tx_info *info = IEEE80211_SKB_CB(skb);
	u8 sec_type = 0;

	if (info && info->control.hw_key) {
		struct ieee80211_key_conf *key = info->control.hw_key;

		switch (key->cipher) {
		case WLAN_CIPHER_SUITE_WEP40:
		case WLAN_CIPHER_SUITE_WEP104:
		case WLAN_CIPHER_SUITE_TKIP:
			sec_type = 0x01;
			break;
		case WLAN_CIPHER_SUITE_CCMP:
			sec_type = 0x03;
			break;
		default:
			break;
		}
	}

	pkt_info->sec_type = sec_type;
}

static void rtw_tx_mgmt_pkt_info_update(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
					struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
					struct ieee80211_sta *sta,
					struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	rtw_tx_pkt_info_update_rate(rtwdev, pkt_info, skb, false);
	pkt_info->dis_qselseq = true;
	pkt_info->en_hwseq = true;
	pkt_info->hw_ssn_sel = 0;
	/* TODO: need to change hw port and hw ssn sel for multiple vifs */
}

static void rtw_tx_data_pkt_info_update(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
					struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
					struct ieee80211_sta *sta,
					struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct ieee80211_hdr *hdr = (struct ieee80211_hdr *)skb->data;
	struct ieee80211_tx_info *info = IEEE80211_SKB_CB(skb);
	struct ieee80211_hw *hw = rtwdev->hw;
	struct rtw_dm_info *dm_info = &rtwdev->dm_info;
	struct rtw_sta_info *si;
	u8 fix_rate;
	u16 seq;
	u8 ampdu_factor = 0;
	u8 ampdu_density = 0;
	bool ampdu_en = false;
	u8 rate = DESC_RATE6M;
	u8 rate_id = 6;
	u8 bw = RTW_CHANNEL_WIDTH_20;
	bool stbc = false;
	bool ldpc = false;

	seq = (le16_to_cpu(hdr->seq_ctrl) & IEEE80211_SCTL_SEQ) >> 4;

	/* for broadcast/multicast, use default values */
	if (!sta)
		goto out;

	if (info->flags & IEEE80211_TX_CTL_AMPDU) {
		ampdu_en = true;
		ampdu_factor = get_tx_ampdu_factor(sta);
		ampdu_density = get_tx_ampdu_density(sta);
	}

	if (info->control.use_rts || skb->len > hw->wiphy->rts_threshold)
		pkt_info->rts = true;

	if (sta->deflink.vht_cap.vht_supported)
		rate = get_highest_vht_tx_rate(rtwdev, sta);
	else if (sta->deflink.ht_cap.ht_supported)
		rate = get_highest_ht_tx_rate(rtwdev, sta);
	else if (sta->deflink.supp_rates[0] <= 0xf)
		rate = DESC_RATE11M;
	else
		rate = DESC_RATE54M;

	si = (struct rtw_sta_info *)sta->drv_priv;

	bw = si->bw_mode;
	rate_id = si->rate_id;
	stbc = rtwdev->hal.txrx_1ss ? false : si->stbc_en;
	ldpc = si->ldpc_en;

out:
	pkt_info->seq = seq;
	pkt_info->ampdu_factor = ampdu_factor;
	pkt_info->ampdu_density = ampdu_density;
	pkt_info->ampdu_en = ampdu_en;
	pkt_info->rate = rate;
	pkt_info->rate_id = rate_id;
	pkt_info->bw = bw;
	pkt_info->stbc = stbc;
	pkt_info->ldpc = ldpc;

	fix_rate = dm_info->fix_rate;
	if (fix_rate < DESC_RATE_MAX) {
		pkt_info->rate = fix_rate;
		pkt_info->dis_rate_fallback = true;
		pkt_info->use_rate = true;
	}
}

/* Direct AWDL peers do not have the infrastructure AP's firmware RA table.
 * Match our unencrypted TX envelopes before selecting a conservative OFDM
 * rate. Never apply this to ordinary ToDS traffic or arbitrary management. */
static bool rtw_tx_is_awdl(const struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	static const u8 bssid[6] = {0, 0x25, 0, 0xff, 0x94, 0x73};
	static const u8 action[6] = {127, 0, 0x17, 0xf2, 8, 0x10};
	static const u8 snap[10] = {0xaa, 0xaa, 3, 0, 0x17, 0xf2, 8, 0, 3, 4};
	const u8 *p = skb->data;

	if (skb->len < 40 || (p[1] & 0xc7) || memcmp(p + 16, bssid, 6))
		return false;
	if (p[0] == 0xd0)
		return !memcmp(p + 24, action, sizeof(action)) &&
		       (p[30] == 0 || p[30] == 3);
	return p[0] == 0x08 && !memcmp(p + 24, snap, sizeof(snap));
}

static void rtw_tx_awdl_rate(struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info)
{
	pkt_info->rate = DESC_RATE6M;
	pkt_info->rate_id = RTW_RATEID_G;
	pkt_info->bw = RTW_CHANNEL_WIDTH_20;
	pkt_info->use_rate = true;
	pkt_info->dis_rate_fallback = true;
}

void rtw_tx_pkt_info_update(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
			    struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
			    struct ieee80211_sta *sta,
			    struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	struct ieee80211_tx_info *info = IEEE80211_SKB_CB(skb);
	struct ieee80211_hdr *hdr = (struct ieee80211_hdr *)skb->data;
	struct ieee80211_vif *vif = info->control.vif;
	struct rtw_sta_info *si;
	struct rtw_vif *rtwvif;
	__le16 fc = hdr->frame_control;
	bool bmc;

	if (sta) {
		si = (struct rtw_sta_info *)sta->drv_priv;
		pkt_info->mac_id = si->mac_id;
	} else if (vif) {
		rtwvif = (struct rtw_vif *)vif->drv_priv;
		pkt_info->mac_id = rtwvif->mac_id;
	}

	if (ieee80211_is_mgmt(fc) || ieee80211_is_nullfunc(fc))
		rtw_tx_mgmt_pkt_info_update(rtwdev, pkt_info, sta, skb);
	else if (ieee80211_is_data(fc))
		rtw_tx_data_pkt_info_update(rtwdev, pkt_info, sta, skb);

	/* Management otherwise defaults to CCK on channel 6. Direct data without
	 * a peer STA otherwise leaves use_rate clear and inherits AP firmware RA.
	 * Pin only AWDL; infrastructure Wi-Fi retains its normal rate adaptation. */
	if (!sta && rtw_tx_is_awdl(skb))
		rtw_tx_awdl_rate(pkt_info);

	bmc = is_broadcast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1) ||
	      is_multicast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1);

	if (info->flags & IEEE80211_TX_CTL_REQ_TX_STATUS)
		rtw_tx_report_enable(rtwdev, pkt_info);

	pkt_info->bmc = bmc;
	rtw_tx_pkt_info_update_sec(rtwdev, pkt_info, skb);
	pkt_info->tx_pkt_size = skb->len;
	pkt_info->offset = chip->tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	pkt_info->qsel = skb->priority;
	pkt_info->ls = true;

	/* maybe merge with tx status ? */
	rtw_tx_stats(rtwdev, vif, skb);
}

void rtw_tx_rsvd_page_pkt_info_update(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				      struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
				      struct sk_buff *skb,
				      enum rtw_rsvd_packet_type type)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	struct ieee80211_hdr *hdr = (struct ieee80211_hdr *)skb->data;
	bool bmc;

	/* A beacon or dummy reserved page packet indicates that it is the first
	 * reserved page, and the qsel of it will be set in each hci.
	 */
	if (type != RSVD_BEACON && type != RSVD_DUMMY)
		pkt_info->qsel = TX_DESC_QSEL_MGMT;

	rtw_tx_pkt_info_update_rate(rtwdev, pkt_info, skb, true);

	bmc = is_broadcast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1) ||
	      is_multicast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1);
	pkt_info->bmc = bmc;
	pkt_info->tx_pkt_size = skb->len;
	pkt_info->offset = chip->tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	pkt_info->ls = true;
	if (type == RSVD_PS_POLL) {
		pkt_info->nav_use_hdr = true;
	} else {
		pkt_info->dis_qselseq = true;
		pkt_info->en_hwseq = true;
		pkt_info->hw_ssn_sel = 0;
	}
	if (type == RSVD_QOS_NULL)
		pkt_info->bt_null = true;

	if (type == RSVD_BEACON) {
		struct rtw_rsvd_page *rsvd_pkt;
		int hdr_len;

		rsvd_pkt = list_first_entry_or_null(&rtwdev->rsvd_page_list,
						    struct rtw_rsvd_page,
						    build_list);
		if (rsvd_pkt && rsvd_pkt->tim_offset != 0) {
			hdr_len = sizeof(struct ieee80211_hdr_3addr);
			pkt_info->tim_offset = rsvd_pkt->tim_offset - hdr_len;
		}
	}

	rtw_tx_pkt_info_update_sec(rtwdev, pkt_info, skb);

	/* TODO: need to change hw port and hw ssn sel for multiple vifs */
}

struct sk_buff *
rtw_tx_write_data_rsvd_page_get(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
				u8 *buf, u32 size)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	struct sk_buff *skb;
	u32 tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	u32 length;

	tx_pkt_desc_sz = chip->tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	length = size + tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	skb = dev_alloc_skb(length);
	if (!skb) {
		rtw_err(rtwdev, "failed to alloc write data rsvd page skb\n");
		return NULL;
	}

	skb_reserve(skb, tx_pkt_desc_sz);
	skb_put_data(skb, buf, size);
	rtw_tx_rsvd_page_pkt_info_update(rtwdev, pkt_info, skb, RSVD_BEACON);

	return skb;
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(rtw_tx_write_data_rsvd_page_get);

struct sk_buff *
rtw_tx_write_data_h2c_get(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
			  struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
			  u8 *buf, u32 size)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	struct sk_buff *skb;
	u32 tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	u32 length;

	tx_pkt_desc_sz = chip->tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	length = size + tx_pkt_desc_sz;
	skb = dev_alloc_skb(length);
	if (!skb) {
		rtw_err(rtwdev, "failed to alloc write data h2c skb\n");
		return NULL;
	}

	skb_reserve(skb, tx_pkt_desc_sz);
	skb_put_data(skb, buf, size);
	pkt_info->tx_pkt_size = size;

	return skb;
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(rtw_tx_write_data_h2c_get);

void rtw_tx(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
	    struct ieee80211_tx_control *control,
	    struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct rtw_tx_pkt_info pkt_info = {0};
	int ret;

	rtw_tx_pkt_info_update(rtwdev, &pkt_info, control->sta, skb);
	ret = rtw_hci_tx_write(rtwdev, &pkt_info, skb);
	if (ret) {
		rtw_err(rtwdev, "failed to write TX skb to HCI\n");
		goto out;
	}

	rtw_hci_tx_kick_off(rtwdev);

	return;

out:
	ieee80211_free_txskb(rtwdev->hw, skb);
}

static void rtw_txq_check_agg(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
			      struct rtw_txq *rtwtxq,
			      struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct ieee80211_txq *txq = rtwtxq_to_txq(rtwtxq);
	struct ieee80211_tx_info *info;
	struct rtw_sta_info *si;

	if (test_bit(RTW_TXQ_AMPDU, &rtwtxq->flags)) {
		info = IEEE80211_SKB_CB(skb);
		info->flags |= IEEE80211_TX_CTL_AMPDU;
		return;
	}

	if (skb_get_queue_mapping(skb) == IEEE80211_AC_VO)
		return;

	if (test_bit(RTW_TXQ_BLOCK_BA, &rtwtxq->flags))
		return;

	if (unlikely(skb->protocol == cpu_to_be16(ETH_P_PAE)))
		return;

	if (!txq->sta)
		return;

	si = (struct rtw_sta_info *)txq->sta->drv_priv;
	set_bit(txq->tid, si->tid_ba);

	ieee80211_queue_work(rtwdev->hw, &rtwdev->ba_work);
}

static int rtw_txq_push_skb(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
			    struct rtw_txq *rtwtxq,
			    struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct ieee80211_txq *txq = rtwtxq_to_txq(rtwtxq);
	struct rtw_tx_pkt_info pkt_info = {0};
	int ret;

	rtw_txq_check_agg(rtwdev, rtwtxq, skb);

	rtw_tx_pkt_info_update(rtwdev, &pkt_info, txq->sta, skb);
	ret = rtw_hci_tx_write(rtwdev, &pkt_info, skb);
	if (ret) {
		rtw_err(rtwdev, "failed to write TX skb to HCI\n");
		return ret;
	}
	return 0;
}

static struct sk_buff *rtw_txq_dequeue(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				       struct rtw_txq *rtwtxq)
{
	struct ieee80211_txq *txq = rtwtxq_to_txq(rtwtxq);
	struct sk_buff *skb;

	skb = ieee80211_tx_dequeue(rtwdev->hw, txq);
	if (!skb)
		return NULL;

	return skb;
}

static void rtw_txq_push(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
			 struct rtw_txq *rtwtxq,
			 unsigned long frames)
{
	struct sk_buff *skb;
	int ret;
	int i;

	rcu_read_lock();

	for (i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
		skb = rtw_txq_dequeue(rtwdev, rtwtxq);
		if (!skb)
			break;

		ret = rtw_txq_push_skb(rtwdev, rtwtxq, skb);
		if (ret) {
			rtw_err(rtwdev, "failed to pusk skb, ret %d\n", ret);
			break;
		}
	}

	rcu_read_unlock();
}

void __rtw_tx_work(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev)
{
	struct rtw_txq *rtwtxq, *tmp;

	spin_lock_bh(&rtwdev->txq_lock);

	list_for_each_entry_safe(rtwtxq, tmp, &rtwdev->txqs, list) {
		struct ieee80211_txq *txq = rtwtxq_to_txq(rtwtxq);
		unsigned long frame_cnt;

		ieee80211_txq_get_depth(txq, &frame_cnt, NULL);
		rtw_txq_push(rtwdev, rtwtxq, frame_cnt);

		list_del_init(&rtwtxq->list);
	}

	rtw_hci_tx_kick_off(rtwdev);

	spin_unlock_bh(&rtwdev->txq_lock);
}

void rtw_tx_work(struct work_struct *w)
{
	struct rtw_dev *rtwdev = container_of(w, struct rtw_dev, tx_work);

	__rtw_tx_work(rtwdev);
}

void rtw_txq_init(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, struct ieee80211_txq *txq)
{
	struct rtw_txq *rtwtxq;

	if (!txq)
		return;

	rtwtxq = (struct rtw_txq *)txq->drv_priv;
	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&rtwtxq->list);
}

void rtw_txq_cleanup(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, struct ieee80211_txq *txq)
{
	struct rtw_txq *rtwtxq;

	if (!txq)
		return;

	rtwtxq = (struct rtw_txq *)txq->drv_priv;
	spin_lock_bh(&rtwdev->txq_lock);
	if (!list_empty(&rtwtxq->list))
		list_del_init(&rtwtxq->list);
	spin_unlock_bh(&rtwdev->txq_lock);
}

static const enum rtw_tx_queue_type ac_to_hwq[] = {
	[IEEE80211_AC_VO] = RTW_TX_QUEUE_VO,
	[IEEE80211_AC_VI] = RTW_TX_QUEUE_VI,
	[IEEE80211_AC_BE] = RTW_TX_QUEUE_BE,
	[IEEE80211_AC_BK] = RTW_TX_QUEUE_BK,
};

static_assert(ARRAY_SIZE(ac_to_hwq) == IEEE80211_NUM_ACS);

enum rtw_tx_queue_type rtw_tx_ac_to_hwq(enum ieee80211_ac_numbers ac)
{
	if (WARN_ON(unlikely(ac >= IEEE80211_NUM_ACS)))
		return RTW_TX_QUEUE_BE;

	return ac_to_hwq[ac];
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(rtw_tx_ac_to_hwq);

enum rtw_tx_queue_type rtw_tx_queue_mapping(struct sk_buff *skb)
{
	struct ieee80211_hdr *hdr = (struct ieee80211_hdr *)skb->data;
	__le16 fc = hdr->frame_control;
	u8 q_mapping = skb_get_queue_mapping(skb);
	enum rtw_tx_queue_type queue;

	if (unlikely(ieee80211_is_beacon(fc)))
		queue = RTW_TX_QUEUE_BCN;
	else if (unlikely(ieee80211_is_mgmt(fc) || ieee80211_is_ctl(fc)))
		queue = RTW_TX_QUEUE_MGMT;
	else if (is_broadcast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1) ||
		 is_multicast_ether_addr(hdr->addr1))
		queue = RTW_TX_QUEUE_HI0;
	else if (WARN_ON_ONCE(q_mapping >= ARRAY_SIZE(ac_to_hwq)))
		queue = ac_to_hwq[IEEE80211_AC_BE];
	else
		queue = ac_to_hwq[q_mapping];

	return queue;
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(rtw_tx_queue_mapping);

static inline
void fill_txdesc_checksum_common(u8 *txdesc, size_t words)
{
	__le16 chksum = 0;
	__le16 *data = (__le16 *)(txdesc);
	struct rtw_tx_desc *tx_desc = (struct rtw_tx_desc *)txdesc;

	le32p_replace_bits(&tx_desc->w7, 0, RTW_TX_DESC_W7_TXDESC_CHECKSUM);

	while (words--)
		chksum ^= *data++;

	le32p_replace_bits(&tx_desc->w7, __le16_to_cpu(chksum),
			   RTW_TX_DESC_W7_TXDESC_CHECKSUM);
}

static inline void rtw_tx_fill_txdesc_checksum(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
					       struct rtw_tx_pkt_info *pkt_info,
					       u8 *txdesc)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;

	chip->ops->fill_txdesc_checksum(rtwdev, pkt_info, txdesc);
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
/* Copyright(c) 2018-2019  Realtek Corporation
 */

#include "main.h"
#include "util.h"
#include "reg.h"

bool check_hw_ready(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, u32 addr, u32 mask, u32 target)
{
	u32 cnt;

	for (cnt = 0; cnt < 1000; cnt++) {
		if (rtw_read32_mask(rtwdev, addr, mask) == target)
			return true;

		udelay(10);
	}

	return false;
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(check_hw_ready);

bool ltecoex_read_reg(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, u16 offset, u32 *val)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	const struct rtw_ltecoex_addr *ltecoex = chip->ltecoex_addr;

	if (!check_hw_ready(rtwdev, ltecoex->ctrl, LTECOEX_READY, 1))
		return false;

	rtw_write32(rtwdev, ltecoex->ctrl, 0x800F0000 | offset);
	*val = rtw_read32(rtwdev, ltecoex->rdata);

	return true;
}

bool ltecoex_reg_write(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, u16 offset, u32 value)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	const struct rtw_ltecoex_addr *ltecoex = chip->ltecoex_addr;

	if (!check_hw_ready(rtwdev, ltecoex->ctrl, LTECOEX_READY, 1))
		return false;

	rtw_write32(rtwdev, ltecoex->wdata, value);
	rtw_write32(rtwdev, ltecoex->ctrl, 0xC00F0000 | offset);

	return true;
}

void rtw_restore_reg(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
		     struct rtw_backup_info *bckp, u32 num)
{
	u8 len;
	u32 reg;
	u32 val;
	int i;

	for (i = 0; i < num; i++, bckp++) {
		len = bckp->len;
		reg = bckp->reg;
		val = bckp->val;

		switch (len) {
		case 1:
			rtw_write8(rtwdev, reg, (u8)val);
			break;
		case 2:
			rtw_write16(rtwdev, reg, (u16)val);
			break;
		case 4:
			rtw_write32(rtwdev, reg, (u32)val);
			break;
		default:
			break;
		}
	}
}
EXPORT_SYMBOL(rtw_restore_reg);

void rtw_desc_to_mcsrate(u16 rate, u8 *mcs, u8 *nss)
{
	if (rate <= DESC_RATE54M)
		return;

	if (rate >= DESC_RATEVHT1SS_MCS0 &&
	    rate <= DESC_RATEVHT1SS_MCS9) {
		*nss = 1;
		*mcs = rate - DESC_RATEVHT1SS_MCS0;
	} else if (rate >= DESC_RATEVHT2SS_MCS0 &&
		   rate <= DESC_RATEVHT2SS_MCS9) {
		*nss = 2;
		*mcs = rate - DESC_RATEVHT2SS_MCS0;
	} else if (rate >= DESC_RATEVHT3SS_MCS0 &&
		   rate <= DESC_RATEVHT3SS_MCS9) {
		*nss = 3;
		*mcs = rate - DESC_RATEVHT3SS_MCS0;
	} else if (rate >= DESC_RATEVHT4SS_MCS0 &&
		   rate <= DESC_RATEVHT4SS_MCS9) {
		*nss = 4;
		*mcs = rate - DESC_RATEVHT4SS_MCS0;
	} else if (rate >= DESC_RATEMCS0 &&
		   rate <= DESC_RATEMCS31) {
		*nss = 0;
		*mcs = rate - DESC_RATEMCS0;
	}
}

struct rtw_stas_entry {
	struct list_head list;
	struct ieee80211_sta *sta;
};

struct rtw_iter_stas_data {
	struct rtw_dev *rtwdev;
	struct list_head list;
};

static void rtw_collect_sta_iter(void *data, struct ieee80211_sta *sta)
{
	struct rtw_iter_stas_data *iter_stas = data;
	struct rtw_stas_entry *stas_entry;

	stas_entry = kmalloc_obj(*stas_entry, GFP_ATOMIC);
	if (!stas_entry)
		return;

	stas_entry->sta = sta;
	list_add_tail(&stas_entry->list, &iter_stas->list);
}

void rtw_iterate_stas(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
		      void (*iterator)(void *data,
				       struct ieee80211_sta *sta),
		      void *data)
{
	struct rtw_iter_stas_data iter_data;
	struct rtw_stas_entry *sta_entry, *tmp;

	/* &rtwdev->mutex makes sure no stations can be removed between
	 * collecting the stations and iterating over them.
	 */
	lockdep_assert_held(&rtwdev->mutex);

	iter_data.rtwdev = rtwdev;
	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&iter_data.list);

	ieee80211_iterate_stations_atomic(rtwdev->hw, rtw_collect_sta_iter,
					  &iter_data);

	list_for_each_entry_safe(sta_entry, tmp, &iter_data.list,
				 list) {
		list_del_init(&sta_entry->list);
		iterator(data, sta_entry->sta);
		kfree(sta_entry);
	}
}

struct rtw_vifs_entry {
	struct list_head list;
	struct ieee80211_vif *vif;
};

struct rtw_iter_vifs_data {
	struct rtw_dev *rtwdev;
	struct list_head list;
};

static void rtw_collect_vif_iter(void *data, u8 *mac, struct ieee80211_vif *vif)
{
	struct rtw_iter_vifs_data *iter_stas = data;
	struct rtw_vifs_entry *vifs_entry;

	vifs_entry = kmalloc_obj(*vifs_entry, GFP_ATOMIC);
	if (!vifs_entry)
		return;

	vifs_entry->vif = vif;
	list_add_tail(&vifs_entry->list, &iter_stas->list);
}

void rtw_iterate_vifs(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
		      void (*iterator)(void *data, struct ieee80211_vif *vif),
		      void *data)
{
	struct rtw_iter_vifs_data iter_data;
	struct rtw_vifs_entry *vif_entry, *tmp;

	/* &rtwdev->mutex makes sure no interfaces can be removed between
	 * collecting the interfaces and iterating over them.
	 */
	lockdep_assert_held(&rtwdev->mutex);

	iter_data.rtwdev = rtwdev;
	INIT_LIST_HEAD(&iter_data.list);

	ieee80211_iterate_active_interfaces_atomic(rtwdev->hw,
						   IEEE80211_IFACE_ITER_NORMAL,
						   rtw_collect_vif_iter, &iter_data);

	list_for_each_entry_safe(vif_entry, tmp, &iter_data.list,
				 list) {
		list_del_init(&vif_entry->list);
		iterator(data, vif_entry->vif);
		kfree(vif_entry);
	}
}


=== FILE tests/rtw88_skb_tx_completion_test.cpp SHA256=bd3ea69e385f1da959a36d22e01e277807c96300bfede1ab9f0c478bf0c5d368 BYTES=4216 ===
COMPLETION_OWNERSHIP_LINES: 8,19,31,37,39,40,47,49,53,54,55,64,68,69,76,78,82,87,106,110,112,114,115,117,126,128
----- COMPLETE SOURCE -----
#include <atomic>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

extern "C" {
#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/compat/linux/skbuff_tx_completion.h"
}

struct Capture {
    std::atomic<unsigned> calls{0};
    std::atomic<unsigned> sent{0};
    std::atomic<unsigned> dropped{0};
    std::atomic<unsigned> releases{0};
    void *expectedCookie = nullptr;
};

static void completion(void *context, void *cookie, bool transmitted)
{
    auto *capture = static_cast<Capture *>(context);
    assert(capture != nullptr);
    assert(cookie == capture->expectedCookie);
    capture->calls.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    (transmitted ? capture->sent : capture->dropped)
        .fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

static void releaseToken(void *opaque)
{
    auto *token = static_cast<rtw88_skb_tx_completion_token *>(opaque);
    auto *capture = static_cast<Capture *>(token->context);
    capture->releases.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    delete token;
}

static rtw88_skb_tx_completion_token *makeToken(Capture *capture, void *cookie)
{
    auto *token = new rtw88_skb_tx_completion_token{};
    token->callback = completion;
    token->release = releaseToken;
    token->context = capture;
    token->cookie = cookie;
    return token;
}

static void test_normal_completion_is_exactly_once()
{
    rtw88_skb_tx_completion state{};
    Capture capture;
    int cookie = 7;
    capture.expectedCookie = &cookie;
    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_bind(&state, makeToken(&capture, &cookie)));
    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_report(&state, true));
    assert(!rtw88_skb_tx_completion_report(&state, false));
    assert(capture.calls.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.sent.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.dropped.load() == 0);
    assert(capture.releases.load() == 1);
}

static void test_early_free_reports_drop()
{
    rtw88_skb_tx_completion state{};
    Capture capture;
    int cookie = 8;
    capture.expectedCookie = &cookie;
    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_bind(&state, makeToken(&capture, &cookie)));
    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_report(&state, false)); // kfree_skb path
    assert(capture.calls.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.sent.load() == 0);
    assert(capture.dropped.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.releases.load() == 1);
}

static void test_completion_free_race_has_one_winner()
{
    rtw88_skb_tx_completion state{};
    Capture capture;
    int cookie = 9;
    capture.expectedCookie = &cookie;
    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_bind(&state, makeToken(&capture, &cookie)));

    std::vector<std::thread> racers;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 32; ++i) {
        racers.emplace_back([&state, i]() {
            (void)rtw88_skb_tx_completion_report(&state, (i & 1U) != 0);
        });
    }
    for (auto &thread : racers)
        thread.join();

    assert(capture.calls.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.sent.load() + capture.dropped.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.releases.load() == 1);
}

/*
 * Model the critical lifetime race: the TX-status path claims the detached
 * token, then the competing kfree path claims nothing and is free to reclaim
 * the skb's embedded state before callback dispatch. Dispatch must use only
 * the independent token, never dereference the deleted skb state.
 */
static void test_claimed_token_outlives_skb_state()
{
    auto *state = new rtw88_skb_tx_completion{};
    Capture capture;
    int cookie = 10;
    capture.expectedCookie = &cookie;
    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_bind(state, makeToken(&capture, &cookie)));

    auto *claimed = rtw88_skb_tx_completion_claim(state);
    assert(claimed != nullptr);
    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_claim(state) == nullptr); // concurrent free loses
    delete state; // equivalent to kfree_skb reclaiming the skb

    assert(rtw88_skb_tx_completion_dispatch(claimed, true));
    assert(capture.calls.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.sent.load() == 1);
    assert(capture.dropped.load() == 0);
    assert(capture.releases.load() == 1);
}

int main()
{
    test_normal_completion_is_exactly_once();
    test_early_free_reports_drop();
    test_completion_free_race_has_one_winner();
    test_claimed_token_outlives_skb_state();
    return 0;
}


=== FILE tests/wcl-event-ring-test.cpp SHA256=5ac782ed9b46c95d146db2bfd4aeac5dd40f9e0292afb2e443d31db5d781c8d0 BYTES=2572 ===

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
 * Host-side tests for the allocation-free WCL event ring.
 */
#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/RTW88WCLRadioEventRing.hpp"

#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>

struct TestEvent {
    uint32_t type;
    uint32_t generation;
    uint32_t value;
};

int main()
{
    RTW88WCLRadioEventRing<TestEvent, 4> ring;
    TestEvent out = {};

    assert(ring.empty());
    assert(!ring.pop(&out));
    assert(!ring.pop(nullptr));

    assert(ring.push({1, 1, 10}));
    assert(ring.push({2, 1, 20}));
    assert(ring.push({3, 1, 30}));
    assert(ring.push({4, 1, 40}));
    assert(ring.full());
    assert(!ring.push({5, 1, 50}));

    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 1 && out.value == 10);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 2 && out.value == 20);

    /* Wrap the tail, then verify logical remove preserves FIFO order. */
    assert(ring.push({5, 2, 50}));
    assert(ring.push({6, 2, 60}));
    assert(ring.size() == 4);
    assert(ring.removeAt(1));
    assert(ring.size() == 3);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 3);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 5);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 6);
    assert(ring.empty());

    /* replaceLast only coalesces adjacent telemetry, preserving ordering
     * around state-transition events. */
    assert(ring.push({7, 9, 70}));
    const bool replaced = ring.replaceLast(
        [](const TestEvent &event) { return event.type == 7; },
        TestEvent{7, 9, 99});
    assert(replaced);
    assert(ring.push({8, 9, 80}));
    const bool didNotCrossTransition = ring.replaceLast(
        [](const TestEvent &event) { return event.type == 7; },
        TestEvent{7, 9, 100});
    assert(!didNotCrossTransition);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 7 && out.value == 99);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 8 && out.value == 80);

    /* removeFirst can discard an advisory item to reserve room for a
     * higher-priority association/disconnect event. */
    assert(ring.push({9, 1, 1}));
    assert(ring.push({10, 1, 2}));
    assert(ring.push({11, 1, 3}));
    assert(ring.push({12, 1, 4}));
    assert(ring.removeFirst([](const TestEvent &event) { return event.type == 10; }));
    assert(ring.push({13, 1, 5}));
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 9);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 11);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 12);
    assert(ring.pop(&out) && out.type == 13);

    ring.clear();
    assert(ring.empty());
    std::puts("WCL event ring: all tests passed");
    return 0;
}


=== FILE tests/wcl-request-decoder-test.cpp SHA256=c9d94710f77d60eac9832e19bde14424304a7336fb7037f2f2d418f1dee64241 BYTES=4745 ===
SKYWALK_INTEGRATION_LINES: 7,65

#include <assert.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <vector>

#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLRequestDecoder.hpp"

static void put16(uint8_t *p, uint16_t v)
{
    p[0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v);
    p[1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v >> 8);
}

static void put32(uint8_t *p, uint32_t v)
{
    p[0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v);
    p[1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v >> 8);
    p[2] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v >> 16);
    p[3] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v >> 24);
}

static std::vector<uint8_t> makeScan()
{
    std::vector<uint8_t> data(0x58 + 12, 0);
    put32(data.data() + 0x00, 7);       // request identifier
    const uint8_t bssid[] = {0x02, 0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44, 0x55};
    memcpy(data.data() + 0x14, bssid, sizeof(bssid));
    put32(data.data() + 0x1c, 3);
    memcpy(data.data() + 0x20, "lab", 3);
    put32(data.data() + 0x40, 1);       // active scan
    put32(data.data() + 0x54, 1);
    put32(data.data() + 0x58 + 4, 36);
    put32(data.data() + 0x58 + 8, 0x10); // 5 GHz
    return data;
}

static std::vector<uint8_t> makeOpenJoin()
{
    std::vector<uint8_t> data(0x218 + 18, 0);
    put32(data.data() + 0x10, 1);       // open-system lower auth
    put32(data.data() + 0x14, 0);       // no upper auth
    put32(data.data() + 0x1c, 3);
    memcpy(data.data() + 0x20, "lab", 3);
    const uint8_t bssid[] = {0x02, 0xaa, 0xbb, 0xcc, 0xdd, 0xee};
    memcpy(data.data() + 0x21c, bssid, sizeof(bssid));
    put32(data.data() + 0x214, 1);      // one candidate
    return data;
}

static std::vector<uint8_t> makeWpa2PmkJoin()
{
    std::vector<uint8_t> data = makeOpenJoin();
    put32(data.data() + 0x14, 1U << 3); // WPA2-PSK
    put32(data.data() + 0x44, 32);
    put32(data.data() + 0x48, 6);       // PMK
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 32; ++i) data[0x50 + i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(i + 1);
    put16(data.data() + 0xd4, 4);
    data[0xd6] = 48; data[0xd7] = 2; data[0xd8] = 1; data[0xd9] = 0;
    return data;
}

int main()
{
    using namespace RTW88WCLRequest;

    Scan scan = {};
    std::vector<uint8_t> scanBytes = makeScan();
    assert(decodeScan(scanBytes.data(), scanBytes.size(), scan));
    assert(scan.identifier == 7 && scan.type == 1 && scan.ssidLength == 3);
    assert(memcmp(scan.ssid, "lab", 3) == 0);
    assert(scan.channelBitmap[36 / 8] & (1U << (36 % 8)));
    assert(!decodeScan(scanBytes.data(), 0x57, scan));

    auto badScan = scanBytes;
    put32(badScan.data() + 0x54, 401);
    assert(!decodeScan(badScan.data(), badScan.size(), scan));
    badScan = scanBytes;
    put32(badScan.data() + 0x40, 0);
    assert(!decodeScan(badScan.data(), badScan.size(), scan));

    Join join = {};
    auto open = makeOpenJoin();
    assert(decodeJoin(open.data(), open.size(), join));
    assert(join.upperAuth == 0 && join.keyLength == 0 && join.ssidLength == 3);

    auto wpa2 = makeWpa2PmkJoin();
    assert(decodeJoin(wpa2.data(), wpa2.size(), join));
    assert(join.upperAuth == (1U << 3) && join.keyLength == 32);
    assert(join.rsnLength == 4 && join.rsn[0] == 48);

    auto tooShort = wpa2;
    assert(!decodeJoin(tooShort.data(), 0x217, join));
    auto manyCandidates = wpa2;
    put32(manyCandidates.data() + 0x214, 11);
    assert(!decodeJoin(manyCandidates.data(), manyCandidates.size(), join));
    auto multicastBssid = open;
    multicastBssid[0x21c] = 0x03;
    assert(!decodeJoin(multicastBssid.data(), multicastBssid.size(), join));
    auto passphrase = wpa2;
    put32(passphrase.data() + 0x44, 12);
    put32(passphrase.data() + 0x48, 10);
    assert(!decodeJoin(passphrase.data(), passphrase.size(), join));
    auto wep = open;
    put32(wep.data() + 0x14, 0);
    put32(wep.data() + 0x44, 5);
    put32(wep.data() + 0x48, 1);
    assert(!decodeJoin(wep.data(), wep.size(), join));

    auto enterprise = wpa2;
    put32(enterprise.data() + 0x14, 1U << 2); // WPA2-802.1X is not implemented
    assert(!decodeJoin(enterprise.data(), enterprise.size(), join));
    auto sha256Psk = wpa2;
    put32(sha256Psk.data() + 0x14, 1U << 7); // SHA256-PSK not implemented
    assert(!decodeJoin(sha256Psk.data(), sha256Psk.size(), join));
    auto noPmk = wpa2;
    put32(noPmk.data() + 0x44, 0);
    assert(!decodeJoin(noPmk.data(), noPmk.size(), join));
    auto badPmkCipher = wpa2;
    put32(badPmkCipher.data() + 0x48, 5); // TKIP/AES key material is not a PMK
    assert(!decodeJoin(badPmkCipher.data(), badPmkCipher.size(), join));
    auto openWithRsn = open;
    put16(openWithRsn.data() + 0xd4, 4);
    openWithRsn[0xd6] = 48; openWithRsn[0xd7] = 2;
    openWithRsn[0xd8] = 1; openWithRsn[0xd9] = 0;
    assert(!decodeJoin(openWithRsn.data(), openWithRsn.size(), join));

    puts("WCL request decoder: all tests passed");
    return 0;
}


=== FILE tests/wcl_mbuf_ring_test.cpp SHA256=0ffd9bf0bf28ffb5922ecfbb4bdfd52bbf5485f3d7c47df7f645d2ad1218f907 BYTES=3340 ===

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only */
#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLMbufRing.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>
#include <sched.h>

static void testCapacityAndWrap()
{
    RTW88WCLMbufRing<void *, 4> ring;
    ring.reset();
    void *items[5] = {
        reinterpret_cast<void *>(uintptr_t(1)),
        reinterpret_cast<void *>(uintptr_t(2)),
        reinterpret_cast<void *>(uintptr_t(3)),
        reinterpret_cast<void *>(uintptr_t(4)),
        reinterpret_cast<void *>(uintptr_t(5))
    };
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
        assert(ring.push(items[i]));
    assert(!ring.push(items[4]));
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        void *out = nullptr;
        assert(ring.pop(&out));
        assert(out == items[i]);
    }
    void *out = nullptr;
    assert(!ring.pop(&out));
    for (uint32_t cycle = 0; cycle < 20000; ++cycle) {
        void *value = reinterpret_cast<void *>(uintptr_t(cycle + 1));
        assert(ring.push(value));
        out = nullptr;
        assert(ring.pop(&out));
        assert(out == value);
    }
}

static void testConcurrentMpmcTraffic()
{
    constexpr uint32_t kProducerCount = 8;
    constexpr uint32_t kItemsPerProducer = 6000;
    constexpr uint32_t kTotal = kProducerCount * kItemsPerProducer;
    RTW88WCLMbufRing<void *, 128> ring;
    ring.reset();

    std::vector<std::atomic<uint32_t>> seen(kTotal);
    for (auto &entry : seen)
        entry.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);

    std::atomic<uint32_t> produced{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> consumed{0};
    std::vector<std::thread> producers;
    producers.reserve(kProducerCount);

    for (uint32_t thread = 0; thread < kProducerCount; ++thread) {
        producers.emplace_back([&, thread] {
            for (uint32_t n = 0; n < kItemsPerProducer; ++n) {
                const uint32_t id = thread * kItemsPerProducer + n;
                void *value = reinterpret_cast<void *>(uintptr_t(id + 1));
                while (!ring.push(value))
                    sched_yield();
                produced.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_release);
            }
        });
    }

    std::vector<std::thread> consumers;
    consumers.emplace_back([&] {
        while (consumed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) < kTotal) {
            void *value = nullptr;
            if (!ring.pop(&value)) {
                sched_yield();
                continue;
            }
            const uintptr_t raw = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(value);
            assert(raw >= 1 && raw <= kTotal);
            const uint32_t id = static_cast<uint32_t>(raw - 1);
            uint32_t expected = 0;
            assert(seen[id].compare_exchange_strong(expected, 1,
                                                    std::memory_order_acq_rel));
            consumed.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_release);
        }
    });

    for (auto &producer : producers)
        producer.join();
    consumers.front().join();

    assert(produced.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == kTotal);
    assert(consumed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == kTotal);
    assert(ring.empty());
    for (const auto &entry : seen)
        assert(entry.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 1);
}

int main()
{
    testCapacityAndWrap();
    testConcurrentMpmcTraffic();
    return 0;
}


=== FILE tests/wcl_packet_span_test.cpp SHA256=cd0affdb1cbcada13cb2be8e31b71183aaac15407d9d035634a2a19d7f855e80 BYTES=1611 ===

#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>

#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLPacketSpanValidation.hpp"

static void test_valid_packet()
{
    const RTW88WCLPacketSpan span{262144, 2048, 2048, 64, 1500};
    assert(RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid(span));
    assert(RTW88WCLIsValidEthernetFrameLength(1514, 1984));
}

static void test_exact_end_boundaries()
{
    const RTW88WCLPacketSpan span{4096, 2048, 2048, 2048, 0};
    assert(RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid(span)); // empty RX buffer at exact end
    assert(RTW88WCLRangeFits(2048, 2048, 0));
    assert(RTW88WCLIsValidEthernetFrameLength(2048, 2048));
}

static void test_rejects_invalid_offsets_and_lengths()
{
    assert(!RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid({4096, 4097, 2048, 0, 1}));
    assert(!RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid({4096, 2048, 2048, 2049, 0}));
    assert(!RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid({4096, 2048, 2048, 2000, 100}));
    assert(!RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid({4096, UINT64_MAX - 3, 2048, 0, 1}));
    assert(!RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid({4096, 0, 0, 0, 0}));
    assert(!RTW88WCLIsValidEthernetFrameLength(13, 2048));
    assert(!RTW88WCLIsValidEthernetFrameLength(2049, 4096));
    assert(!RTW88WCLIsValidEthernetFrameLength(1500, 1499));
}

static void test_range_arithmetic_does_not_overflow()
{
    assert(RTW88WCLRangeFits(UINT64_MAX, UINT64_MAX, 0));
    assert(!RTW88WCLRangeFits(UINT64_MAX, UINT64_MAX, 1));
    assert(!RTW88WCLRangeFits(10, 9, UINT64_MAX));
}

int main()
{
    test_valid_packet();
    test_exact_end_boundaries();
    test_rejects_invalid_offsets_and_lengths();
    test_range_arithmetic_does_not_overflow();
    return 0;
}


=== FILE tests/wcl_registration_info_stage11_test.cpp SHA256=ebf49fc664967aaaad12818de25096d809501f959c2e99a5771301cf9f6f82ac BYTES=2886 ===

#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo.hpp"

#include <stdio.h>

using namespace RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo24H32;

static bool inRange(size_t offset, size_t start, size_t length)
{
    return offset >= start && offset < start + length;
}

int main()
{
    Storage storage;
    memset(storage.bytes, 0xa5, sizeof(storage.bytes));

    const uint8_t address[6] = {0x02, 0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44, 0x55};
    if (!applyAppleProducerWrites(storage, address)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "applyAppleProducerWrites rejected a valid address\n");
        return 1;
    }

    if (loadU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot0C) != kOpaqueValue0C ||
        loadU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot14) != kOpaqueValue14 ||
        loadU64(storage, kOpaqueSlot24) != kOpaqueValue24 ||
        loadU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot40) != kOpaqueValue40 ||
        memcmp(storage.bytes + kHardwareAddress, address, sizeof(address)) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Apple 24H32 opaque writes do not match the audited values\n");
        return 2;
    }

    /* The writer must preserve all bytes not explicitly written by Apple's
     * producer. This protects the opaque values populated by initRegistrationInfo.
     */
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(storage.bytes); ++i) {
        const bool written =
            inRange(i, kOpaqueSlot0C, sizeof(uint32_t)) ||
            inRange(i, kOpaqueSlot14, sizeof(uint32_t)) ||
            inRange(i, kOpaqueSlot24, sizeof(uint64_t)) ||
            inRange(i, kOpaqueSlot40, sizeof(uint32_t)) ||
            inRange(i, kHardwareAddress, sizeof(address));
        if (!written && storage.bytes[i] != 0xa5) {
            fprintf(stderr, "opaque byte %zu was unexpectedly modified\n", i);
            return 3;
        }
    }

    if (matchesApple24H32Template(storage, address)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "template accepted without initRegistrationInfo header\n");
        return 4;
    }

    storeU64(storage, 0, kVersionAndSize);
    if (!matchesApple24H32Template(storage, address)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "template rejected after exact 24H32 header was set\n");
        return 5;
    }
    if (matchesApple24H32Template(storage, nullptr)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "template accepted a null address\n");
        return 6;
    }
    if (applyAppleProducerWrites(storage, nullptr)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "writer accepted a null address\n");
        return 7;
    }

    clear(storage);
    for (uint8_t byte : storage.bytes) {
        if (byte != 0) {
            fprintf(stderr, "clear left a nonzero byte\n");
            return 8;
        }
    }

    puts("PASS: 0x130-byte aligned opaque RegistrationInfo storage");
    puts("PASS: exact 24H32 Apple producer overrides and six-byte address copy");
    puts("PASS: all unlisted initializer-owned bytes remain untouched");
    puts("PASS: version/size header validation and clear");
    return 0;
}


=== FILE tests/wcl_rx_packet_address_test.cpp SHA256=2fb040e0d6967640c74c12b707484c812ea3c50a42b17e2d36ab8972272200da BYTES=1321 ===

#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <limits>

#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLPacketSpanValidation.hpp"

static void test_resolves_valid_rx_span()
{
    uintptr_t address = 0;
    const RTW88WCLPacketSpan span{65536, 4096, 4096, 0, 1514};
    assert(RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(0x100000, span, &address));
    assert(address == 0x101000);
}

static void test_resolves_data_offset()
{
    uintptr_t address = 0;
    const RTW88WCLPacketSpan span{8192, 1024, 4096, 128, 1500};
    assert(RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(0x200000, span, &address));
    assert(address == 0x200480);
}

static void test_rejects_invalid_or_overflowing_address()
{
    uintptr_t address = 0;
    const RTW88WCLPacketSpan valid{8192, 0, 4096, 0, 64};
    assert(!RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(0, valid, &address));
    assert(!RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(
        static_cast<uint64_t>(UINTPTR_MAX) - 8, valid, &address));
    const RTW88WCLPacketSpan invalid{4096, 4090, 16, 0, 64};
    assert(!RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(0x100000, invalid, &address));
    assert(!RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(0x100000, valid, nullptr));
}

int main()
{
    test_resolves_valid_rx_span();
    test_resolves_data_offset();
    test_rejects_invalid_or_overflowing_address();
    return 0;
}


=== FILE tests/wcl_tx_completion_slots_test.cpp SHA256=b3230ef7ce8025a6ea418d0e0ce53d081bf0bccf57ffe14a29ce2a672a2a9b56 BYTES=4078 ===
COMPLETION_OWNERSHIP_LINES: 2,18,30,31,32,33,34,40,42,46,58,75,79,93,96,100
SKYWALK_INTEGRATION_LINES: 2,18,30,31,32,33,34,40,42,46,58,75,79,93,96,100
----- COMPLETE SOURCE -----
/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only */
#include "../driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>
#include <sched.h>

struct WorkItem {
    uint32_t id;
    std::atomic<uint32_t> completed{0};
};

static void testCapacityAndExactlyOnce()
{
    RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4> pool;
    pool.reset();

    int values[5] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    auto *a = pool.reserve(&values[0]);
    auto *b = pool.reserve(&values[1]);
    auto *c = pool.reserve(&values[2]);
    auto *d = pool.reserve(&values[3]);
    assert(a && b && c && d);
    assert(pool.reserve(&values[4]) == nullptr);
    assert(pool.outstandingCount() == 4);

    assert(RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::complete(a, true));
    assert(!RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::complete(a, false));
    assert(RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::complete(b, false));
    assert(RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::complete(c, true));
    assert(RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::complete(d, true));

    uint32_t seen = 0;
    bool sawSuccess = false;
    bool sawDrop = false;
    while (auto *ticket = pool.claimCompleted()) {
        auto *value = static_cast<int *>(RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::packet(ticket));
        assert(value >= &values[0] && value <= &values[3]);
        if (RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::transmitted(ticket))
            sawSuccess = true;
        else
            sawDrop = true;
        RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<4>::finish(ticket);
        ++seen;
    }
    assert(seen == 4 && sawSuccess && sawDrop);
    assert(!pool.hasOutstanding());
}

static void testConcurrentProducersAndConsumer()
{
    constexpr uint32_t kThreads = 12;
    constexpr uint32_t kPerThread = 2500;
    constexpr uint32_t kTotal = kThreads * kPerThread;
    RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<128> pool;
    pool.reset();

    std::vector<WorkItem> work(kTotal);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kTotal; ++i)
        work[i].id = i;

    std::atomic<uint32_t> produced{0};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> drained{0};
    std::vector<std::thread> producers;
    producers.reserve(kThreads);

    for (uint32_t threadIndex = 0; threadIndex < kThreads; ++threadIndex) {
        producers.emplace_back([&, threadIndex] {
            for (uint32_t n = 0; n < kPerThread; ++n) {
                const uint32_t id = threadIndex * kPerThread + n;
                auto *item = &work[id];
                RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<128>::Ticket *ticket = nullptr;
                while (!(ticket = pool.reserve(item)))
                    sched_yield();
                const bool transmitted = ((id * 7U) % 5U) != 0;
                while (!RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<128>::complete(ticket, transmitted))
                    sched_yield();
                produced.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            }
        });
    }

    while (drained.load(std::memory_order_acquire) < kTotal) {
        auto *ticket = pool.claimCompleted();
        if (!ticket) {
            sched_yield();
            continue;
        }
        auto *item = static_cast<WorkItem *>(
            RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<128>::packet(ticket));
        assert(item != nullptr && item->id < kTotal);
        const uint32_t expected = ((item->id * 7U) % 5U) != 0 ? 1U : 0U;
        assert(RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<128>::transmitted(ticket) == (expected != 0));
        uint32_t notCompleted = 0;
        assert(item->completed.compare_exchange_strong(
            notCompleted, 1, std::memory_order_acq_rel));
        RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<128>::finish(ticket);
        drained.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_release);
    }

    for (auto &producer : producers)
        producer.join();
    assert(produced.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == kTotal);
    assert(drained.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == kTotal);
    assert(!pool.hasOutstanding());
    for (const auto &item : work)
        assert(item.completed.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 1);
}

int main()
{
    testCapacityAndExactlyOnce();
    testConcurrentProducersAndConsumer();
    return 0;
}
